# Documentation Map

Content audit: 2026-10-07. This is the single documentation index, not another
per-task log. Regenerate with `node scripts/audit-docs.mjs --write`; validate
with `node scripts/audit-docs.mjs --check`.

## Scope And Limits

- Parsed the complete content of all 427 remaining Markdown files, including fenced-code boundaries, headings, prose, links, status notes and task checkboxes.
- Indexed 93 supporting files by references and the image manifest. Images were not visually reverified; the HTML prototype was not executed.
- The purpose excerpts below come from document content, not just filenames. Original-language wording is retained where it is the available evidence.
- Dated logs, unchecked historical checklists and old successful test runs are not evidence of current implementation or live runtime health.
- Only the requested decision files were removed. Their old content remains recoverable through Git history; it was not copied into another archive.
- No app rebuild, process stop, live provider switch, model-memory configuration change or network verification was performed.

## Where Things Belong

| Kind | What it actually does | Maintenance verdict |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Audit Records (18) | Dated investigation or release-verification evidence | Historical evidence; consolidate summaries without claiming fresh verification |
| Standalone References (5) | Specialized design or investigation | Candidate for moving into its matching existing topic |
| Topic Guides (31) | Feature explanation or operational procedure | Keep one canonical topic; preserve translations |
| Engineering History (3) | Retained project history or documentation policy | Keep concise/frozen; no per-chat continuation logs |
| Release Notes (214) | Historical announcement and change list | Keep release history; not current implementation evidence |
| Implementation Plans (48) | Task-by-task implementation or release recipe | Archive/consolidation candidate after checking unique rationale and open work |
| Design Specifications (29) | Proposed behavior, ownership, constraints and tradeoffs | Keep unique contracts; consolidate with the canonical topic when implemented |
| User Manuals (79) | User-facing instructions | Keep; review UI/version drift separately |

## Redundancy Findings

- 0 exact whole-document duplicate groups after newline normalization.
- 78 language-sibling groups. These are translations, not automatic deletion candidates.
- 26 exact-name plan/spec pairs. A plan describes execution while a specification describes contracts; consolidate only when their distinct information is preserved.
- 24 long paragraphs occur verbatim in more than one document. Shared text can be boilerplate or intentional quotation, not proof that the whole files are redundant.
- The archive and journal repeat investigation narratives by design. Freeze the archive and record a milestone only once; do not generate a file or status transition for each chat continuation.

## Cleanup Order

1. Do not recreate the retired per-fix decision folder.
2. Use existing topic guides for unique, still-applicable architectural rationale.
3. Resolve missing local references against the actual source/history, not guesses.
4. Consolidate completed plan/spec pairs only after checking unique constraints and unresolved work.
5. Keep translated manuals and release history separate from assistant recaps.
6. Freeze historical archives and stop repeated continuation/test-rerun entries.
